## Document 047: 2330台積電 群益

### Page 1

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 9 月 9 日

1 群益投顧 
ASML (ASML US) 與台積電 (2330 TT) 共同推動 High NA EUV 將自現行的 6 吋光罩，
轉移至更大的 12 吋 光 罩。 目標於 2031 年之前建立 12 吋 光 罩 試 產 線(pilot line) ， 並 於
2033 年之前實現微影系統全面就緒。台積電並表示自 2030 年 起 ， 將 在 先 進 製 程 量 產
中導入 ASML 的 High NA  EUV 。台積電與 ASML 共同發起 6 吋光罩轉移至 12 吋光
罩 的 規 格 轉 移 ， 其 戰 略 意 義 在 於 ， 製 訂 規 格 和 標 準 ， 確 保 台 積 電 技 術 領 先 地 位 。 因 此
對 台 積 電 的 投 資 評 等 Buy ，目標價 3,200 元。

研 究 員 ： 陳 俐 妍   
ly.c@capital.com.tw

ASML 與 台 積 電 共 同 推 動 High NA EUV 將 自 現 行 的 6 吋 光 罩 ， 轉 移 至 更 大
的 12 吋 光 罩 ：  
09/07/2026 ASML 與 台 積 電 於 BACUS 國 際 光 學 工 程 學 會(SPIE) 先 進 微 影 技
術 研 討 會 前 宣 布 發 起 產 業 合 作 ， 共 同 推 動High NA EUV 將 自 現 行 的 6 吋光
罩 ， 轉 移 至 更 大 的 12 吋 光 罩 ， 預 期 將 進 一 步 提 升 晶 圓 廠 的 生 產 力 、 降 低 晶
片 製 造 成 本 ， 並 消 除 需 要 2 張 6 吋 光 罩 在 High NA EUV 曝 光 時 進 行 拼 接 的
限 制，使 整 個 半 導 體 產 業 受 惠。此 轉 移 能 讓 先 進 晶 片 製 造 服 務 提 供 者 充 分 利
用 High NA EUV 的 優 勢 ， 更 提 升 未 來 世 代 先 進 晶 片 的 成 本 效 益 。 
目標於 2031 年 之 前 建 立 12 吋 光 罩 試 產 線(pilot line) ，並於 2033 年 之 前 實
現 微 影 系 統 全 面 就 緒 ， 以 支 援 先 進 製 程 量 產 。 台 積 電 並 表 示 自2030 年 起 ，
將 在 先 進 製 程 量 產 中 導 入 ASML 的 High NA 技 術 。  
12 吋光罩 使 High NA 效 率 提 升 ：  
現行 EUV 設備的 NA(Numerical Aperture ， NA) 為 0.33 ， High NA 則 提 高 到
0.55 。 NA 數 值 越 高 ， 解 析 度 也 越 高 、 曝 光 時 能 呈 現 的 細 節 愈 多 。ASML 資
料顯示 High NA 可 將 最 小 解 析 尺 寸 從 現 行 EUV 的約 13 奈 米 進 一 步 縮 小 到
8 奈 米 ， 也 就 是 能 在 晶 圓 上 印 出 更 細 微 的 電 路 圖 形 。 部 分 原 本 需 要2 次 甚 至
更多次曝光才能完成的圖形，也有機會改以單次曝光處理，降低製程複雜
度 、 缺 陷 風 險 與 生 產 周 期 。 
舉 例 來 說 ，0.33 NA EUV 機 台 ， 至 少 需 要 4 張 光 罩 、 分 多 次 曝 光 才 能 完 成
A14 、 A10 金 屬 層 圖 形 ；High NA EUV ， 只 要 1 張 光 罩 就 能 完 成 相 同 圖 形 。
但 解 析 度 提 高 ，High NA 的 光 學 設 計 讓 單 次 可 曝 光 範 圍 縮 小 到 原 本EUV 的
50% ， 製 作 大 顆 晶 片 時 可 能 必 須 分 成2 次 曝 光 ， 再 把 圖 形 拼 接 起 來 。 這 也 是
台積電與 ASML 此 次 推 動 12 吋 光 罩 的 原 因 ， 相 較 現 行 6 吋 光 罩 ， 放 大 光 罩
尺 寸 預 期 能 進 一 步 提 升 晶 圓 廠 生 產 力、降 低 晶 片 製 造 成 本，並 消 除 High NA
曝 光 時 的 拼 接 限 制 。 
台 積 電 預 計 2030 年 開 始 使 用 High NA  EUV ， 推估 High NA 將 會 在 A10 或
A10 以 下 的 先 進 製 程 採 用：  
台積電預計 2030 年 開 始 導入 High NA  EUV ，比 12 吋 光 罩 微 影 系 統 預 計 全
面就緒的 2033 年 更 早 。 這 代 表 台 積 電 初 期 導 入High NA 時 ， 仍 會 先 使 用 現
行 6 吋 光 罩 ， 之 後 再 隨 12 吋 光 罩 與 相 關 系 統 成 熟 逐 步 轉 換 。

### Page 2

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 9 月 9 日

2 群益投顧 
根 據 台 積 電 目 前 公 開 的 製 程 藍 圖 ，A13 與 A12 製 程 都 預 計 2029 年 量 產 。 其
中 A13 是 A14 平 台 的 延 伸 ，A12 則 將 超 級 電 軌(Super Power Rail) 背面供電
技術導入 A14 平 台 。 因 此 N2 、 A16 、 A14 、 A13 與 A12 都 早 於 台 積 電 預 計
2030 年 導 入 High NA ， 推 估 High NA 將 會 在 A10 或 A10 以 下 的 先 進 製 程 採
用。  
Intel (INTC US) 和 Samsung (005930 KS) 領 先 台 積 電 採 用 High NA EUV ，但
也 同 時 參 與 12 吋 光 罩 開 發 ：  
Intel 在 2024 年 宣 布 完 成 全 球 首 套 商 用 High NA EUV 設 備 的 組 裝 ；2026 年
High NA 已 經 正 式 進 入 量 產 ， 並 用 於 部 分 18A 製 程 層 。 是 目 前 High NA 導
入 進 度 最 快 的 晶 片 製 造 商 。Samsung 則預計 2028 年導入 DRAM 量 產 ； 台 積
電選擇在 2030 年 導 入 。Intel 和 Samsung 也 同 時 表 示 與 ASML 及 供 應 鏈 合
作 ， 推 動 未 來 轉 向 大 尺 寸 光 罩 。 
台 積 電 與 ASML 共 同 發 起 6 吋 光 罩 轉 移 至 12 吋 光 罩 的 規 格 轉 移，將 領 導 業
界 規 格 ：  
6 吋 光 罩 轉 移 至 12 吋光罩 的 這 項 升 級 非 單 純 的 尺 寸 放 大 ， 而 是 面 臨 生 態 系
與 物 理 極 限 的 多 重 挑 戰 。 整 體 供 應 鏈 重 構 ， 包 括 基 板 與 材 料 以 及 設 備 等 等 。
12 吋 光 罩 石 英 基 板 的 平 坦 度 、 缺 陷 密 度 與 熱 膨 脹 係 數 控 制 難 度 倍 增 。 製 程
設 備 如 薄 膜 沉 積、蝕 刻、清 洗 與 自 動 化 搬 運 設 備 皆 需 重 新 開 發。後 續 檢 測 與
量測極限檢測難度也增加，更大面積的圖形缺陷檢測（ Inspection ）與修補
技 術 需 要 突 破 。 在 12 吋 面 積 上 維 持 奈 米 級 的 對 位 精 度 與 線 寬 均 勻 性 極 為 困
難   
電 子 設 計 自 動 化 (EDA) 工 具 必 須 重 新 適 應 大 尺 寸 曝 光 視 場 與 資 料 處 理 。 12 
吋 光 罩 的 圖 形 檔 案 與 運 算 複 雜 度 將 大 幅 上 升 。 
台積電與 ASML 共 同 發 起 6 吋 光 罩 轉 移 至 12 吋 光 罩 的 規 格 轉 移，其 戰略意
義在於 ，台 積 電 為 全 球 最 大 晶 圓 代 工 廠，為 先 進 製 程 最 大 的 供 應 商，可 以 領
導 光 罩 基 底 供 應 商 、 光 罩 檢 測 設 備 商， 甚 至 EDA 廠 等 整 個 生 態 系 ，除 共同
分攤 ， 跨 越 升 級 12 吋 光 罩 的 龐 大 研 發 成 本外 ， 製 訂 規 格 和 標 準 ， 確 保 台 積
電 技 術 領 先 地 位 。因 此 對 台 積 電 的投 資 評 等 Buy ， 目 標 價 3,200 元。

### Page 3

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 9 月 9 日

3 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

## Document 048: 2368金像電 群益

Source PDF: all report pdf/2368金像電 群益.pdf
Report source: 未公開來源
Date: 2026-09-30

### Page 1

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 9 月 30 日

1 群益投顧 
金像電 (2368 TT) 於 09/29/2026 董事會核准總金額 79 億 元 的 資 本 支 出 預 算 案 ， 預 計 自
4Q26 起 陸 續 進 行 投 資，並 決 議 洽 定 特 定 應 募 人 為Amazon.com, Inc(AMZN US) ，實 際
私募價格為 856 元 ， 預 計 此 次 私 募 規 模 為 15.89 億元、占金像電 2Q26 股本 0.36% 。
研 究 部 認 為，金 像 電 作 為 既 有 ASIC 伺 服 器 板 的 生 產 廠 商，隨 著 CSP 業者未來資本支
出 逐 步 放 大 、 對 算 力 的 要 求 也 直 接 提 升 PCB 板 規 格 與 產 品 種 類 ， 金 像 電 也 勢 必 增 加
資 本 支 出 以 維 持 產 業 競 爭 力 。 綜 合 以 上 ， 考 量 新 產 品 、 新 業 務 、 新 產 能 將 持 續 挹 注 金
像電 2027 年的營運，故維持金像電 Buy 的 投 資 建 議 ， 目 標 價 1,480 元 (22.70 PER X 
2027F EPS) 。

研 究 員 ： 吳 建 弘   
A51102@cim.capital.com.tw

金 像 電 09/29 /2026 董 事 會 核 准 資 本 支 出 案 與 公 告 第 一 次 私 募 案 對 象：  
金像電 (2368 TT) 於 09/29/2026 董 事 會 核 准 總 金 額 79 億 元 的 資 本 支 出 預 算
案 ， 預 計 自 4Q26 起 陸 續 進 行 投 資 ， 主 要 進 行 38 億 元 的 土 地 預 算 、41 億元
的 建 廠 與 設 備 資 本 支 出 。 
並決議 2026 年 第 一 次 私 募 普 通 股 定 價 及 應 募 人 資 訊 ，已 洽 定 特 定 應 募 人 為
Amazon.com, Inc(AMZN US) ， 預 計 本 次 私 募 增 資 認 購 股 數 為 1,856,308 股，
實 際 私 募 價 格為 856 元 。 此 次 私 募 規 模 為 15.89 億 元 、 占 金 像 電 2Q26 股本
0.36% 。  
研 究 部 評 析 
參考 04/16/2026 金 像 電 決 議 辦 理 私 募 增 資 案 的 內 容 ， 此 次Amazon 入股，
預 計 將 擴 大金 像 電 的 伺 服 器 業 務 範 圍 ， 提 高 營 運 績 效 。而 金 像 電 作 為既有
ASIC 伺 服 器 板 的 生 產 廠 商 ， 隨 著 CSP 業 者 未 來 資 本 支 出 逐 步 放 大 、 對 算
力 的 要 求 直 接 提 升 PCB 板 規 格 與 產 品 種 類 ， 金 像 電 也 勢 必 增 加 資 本 支 出 以
維 持 產 業 競 爭 力 。 
展望 2027 年 ， 隨 著 新世代的 AI ASIC 伺 服 器 產 品 逐 漸 量 產 ， 新 產 品 、 新
業務 、 新 產 能 將 持 續 挹 注金 像 電 營 運 ， 推 升 後 續 展 望 。 而 09/29/2026 的資
本 支 出 案 ， 研 究 部 基 於 設 備 交 期 規 劃 ， 預 計 將 投 入2028 年後 的 營運。  
綜 合 以 上 ， 考 量新 產 品 、 新 業 務 、 新 產 能 將 持 續 挹 注 金 像 電2027 年的營
運， 故 維 持 金 像 電 Buy 的 投 資 建 議 ， 目 標 價 1,480 元 (22.70 PER X 2027F 
EPS) 。

3 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

### Page 2

Mediatek (2454.TW)
29 September 2026 Citi Research
2
•MediaTek’s advanced-node and supply chain capabilities are underestimated — We believe MediaTek’s Google
TPU order can scale materially into 2028 and beyond despite market concerns regarding possible project delays. The
TPU V8 chip is already in production, and we believe it is on track for US$2bn revenue in 2026. MediaTek has also
consistently been a top 5 customer at TSMC for the most advanced node with big volumes for several years. They

have multiple N2 projects and early work extending toward A14. This matters because leading-edge ASIC execution
is not only about SerDes, on which MediaTek is also catching up with global peers; the RTL design would require
advanced node design and technology co-optimization, physical implementation, foundry capacity management,
package and substrate coordination, testing, and high-volume ramp. Under Google’s COT/semi-custom model, this
capability should become even more relevant as MediaTek provides design, integration, and manufacturing

execution.
•EMIB-T risk for TPU v9, yet CoWoS/CoPoS for v10 likely to succeed — While investors are also skeptical about
MediaTek’s SerDes technology, we note that its 224G SerDes is in production and that 336G and 448G SerDes is
actively under development. MediaTek is also developing die-to-die, which has already been silicon-validated on
TSMC N3P and N2P. For advanced packaging, we would nevertheless not dismiss EMIB-T yield risk for TPU v9 and

large-scale chiplet integration. Yet we believe there is strong chance that MediaTek will be working on
CoWoS/CoPoS for TPU v10, which is aiming for 2029 production. And its higher value for v9 and long lift v8 should
be more than enough to compensate for the potential shipment downside into 2028.
•Growing with expanding TPU opportunity + new customer engagement — There have been reports that Marvell,
AMD, and Samsung Foundry are potential alternatives for high-end HBM4E inference TPU and future TPU V10

designs. In our view, Google’s expanding TPU TAM should support multiple suppliers and TPU V10 is more likely a
2029–2030 generation. We believe MediaTek and TSMC will continue to be the main source. Given MediaTek’s tight
relationship with Nvidia and the NVLink Fusion ecosystem, we believe it has other hyperscale opportunities such as
Microsoft and xAI.
•Maintaining Buy; more than doubling AI revenue into 2028 from 2027 — In our view, Mediatek is emerging as a

credible hyperscale ASIC partner with materially stronger advanced-node, chiplet, SerDes, and supply-chain
capabilities than the bear case implies. EMIB-T and first-generation large-chiplet execution remain the key risks, but
MediaTek’s rapid learning curve, manufacturing scale, cost discipline, and Google’s capacity-sharing model provide
meaningful mechanisms to mitigate them. We estimate MediaTek’s TPU shipment to be at 4m units for 2027 and
2028, but 2028 revenue to double compared to 2027 due to higher ASP on upgraded SerDes (336G) and N2

advanced node top die design effort.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

### Page 4

Mediatek (2454.TW)
29 September 2026 Citi Research
4
Mediatek
Valuation
Our NT$6,800 target price (rounded) for MediaTek is based on 35x P/E to 2027/2028 EPS, which is 1.5-std above its five-year
average forward P/E given the promising outlook from its AI ASIC business starting from 2H26. We believe our target PE is also
justified by Mediatek's global and regional peers' trading at an average of 36x PER. AI ASIC development with CSPs should
provide more upside from 2027 onwards. We also see Mediatek well prepared for 6G and 6G+NTN Satellite communication.

Despite lukewarm smartphone overall deamand, we expect Mediatek to benefit from 5G migration in emerging markets and its
Dimensity series should also gain shares in flagship models. Non-smartphone business is also seeing stabilizing TV and IoT
demand with Mediatek outgrowing the overall market on its better technology in WiFi 7 and connectivity.
Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent the shares from reaching our target price include: 1) weaker-than-expected growth from

AI ASIC business; 2) more severe competition in the smartphone segment; 3) slower overall demand related to macroeconomic
risks, especially for smartphone demand; and 4) further price hikes from foundries.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

E Ink (8069.TWO): E-paper application continues to expand; expect 
modest consumer electronics mix; Aug beat; Neutral
30 September 2026 | 3:59PM HKT
We remain positive on growing e-paper applications across e-readers, e-notes, and 
e-labels. However, consumer electronics end-demand could remain modest amid 
high memory costs. We expect E Ink’s consumer electronics (e-readers, e-notes) to 
decline 13% YoY in 2026E (vs. +6% YoY in 2025), and recover back to +12-16% YoY 
in 2027E-28E; on IoT applications (ESL and Signage), we expect revenue to grow at a 
+32% CAGR in 2026E-28E (vs. +22% YoY in 2025), driven by rising adoption on 
savings of labour costs, electricity, and paper, digitalization trend, and growing ESG 
awareness. Signage new products could also drive revenue growth with high ASPs, 
despite lower GMs considering in module format to accelerate customers’ adoption. 
Our target P/E multiple declines to 11x (vs. 13x previous TP implied 2027E P/E) on 
consumer electronics sector de-rating under high memory costs. Our new 12m TP is 
NT$142 (vs. NT$153 previously). Maintain Neutral.

3-month revenues preview: We expect a sequential increase in revenues through 
Sep-Dec, driven by e-labels, Signage ramp up, and better consumer electronics 
seasonality. The ongoing digitalization should support e-paper adoption; E Ink’s 
e-labels further penetrated in Canada, serving Sobeys (retail) to deploy ESLs across 
300 to 350 stores over the next 18 months (link). Overall, we expect 3Q26E / 4Q26E 
revenues to grow at +0% / +16% QoQ on the mix of modest consumer electronics 
end-demand under high memory costs, and ongoing e-labels adoption and Signage 
shipment ramp up.

### Page 2

Earnings revision

We factor in 2Q26 and Aug results and revise up our 2026E-28E net income by 15% / 
10% / 8% mainly on higher gross margins, given better product mix (e-labels mainly 
delivered via e-paper while consumer electronics mainly via module, carry a lower GM). 
Overall, we expect 2026E revenues growth at +13% YoY (vs. +12% YoY in 2025A), driven 
by e-labels growing application and adoption, which we model to grow +47% YoY (vs. 
+22% YoY in 2025A), while we also expect a decline in consumer electronics under high 
memory costs (-13% YoY vs. +6% YoY in 2025). We continue to expect revenue growth in 
2027E-28E driven by (1) rising color e-notes / e-readers enlarging size, (2) more stores 
adopting e-labels to save operation costs with growing outdoor signage. On GM, we 
increase our forecasts in 2026E-28E to reflect 2Q26 beat on better product mix.

Valuation

We continue to derive our 12m TP based on a near-term P/E. We derive our target P/E 
via E Ink’s avg. 12m forward PEG ratio since 2017. With largely the same PEG ratio of 
0.5x, 22% forward year earnings growth (vs. 26% previously), our new target P/E is at 
11x (vs. 13x our TP implied 2027E P/E previously). The new target P/E of 11x is largely in 
line with the company’s avg.-1stv. P/E, reflecting the consumer electronics sector 
de-rating under high memory costs. Our new 12m target price is NT$142 (vs. NT$153 
previously). Maintain Neutral.

### Page 4

Price target methodology and risks - E Ink 
Valuation methodology: We are Neutral rated on E Ink. Our 12m target price for E Ink is 
based on a near-term P/E, consistent with our Taiwan Technology coverage. Our 12-m 
TP of NT$142 is based on a target P/E multiple of 11x forward year EPS (2027E). Our 
target P/E multiple of 11x is derived from the company’s avg. PEG since 2017 (0.5x), as 
we expect macro uncertainties to reduce the visibility of e-paper adoption, while we 
remain positive in the long term: technology upgrades (colour, larger sizes) to unlock the 
e-paper market potential. 
Upside / Downside risks: (1) Faster- / slower-than-expected e-readers / e-notes 
penetration rate, (2) Faster- / slower-than-expected ESL adoption rate, (3) Fiercer- / 
healthier-than-expected competition, which could be driven by competition from other 
technology gadgets (e.g. AI / AR glasses, AI PCs, tablets, smart watches, etc.) or new 
entrants in e-paper industry.

their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

## Document 064: 261002_daiwa_nypcb

Source PDF: all report pdf/261002_daiwa_nypcb.pdf
Report source: 未公開來源
Date: 2026-10-02
Pages: 4

### Page 1

Summary: NYPCB released its August results (for pre-tax profit and net profit) after 
market hours on 2 October mainly due to recent volatile share price movements. Net profit 
in August 2026 came in at TWD1,092m (EPS: TWD1.69), up 665% YoY, with pre-tax profit 
at TWD1,486m (pre-tax margin at 27.8%). The August pre-tax margin of 27.8% is close to 
our current forecast (28.5%/32.9% in 3Q26E/4Q26E). As we expect limited impact from 
non-ops and on opex in August, we infer the gross margin in August should have reached 
to c.30-31% (vs. our forecast c.30.3/34.4% in 3Q26E/4Q26E). This echoes our previous 
notes (Spec upgrade to drive additional ABF substrate demand); we expect NYPCB’s 
gross margin will recover to c.30% after Kunshan’s yield rate improvement in August. As 
such, we believe the August results to be largely on track with market expectations. 
However, we believe the share price upside will come from BT substrate, where we should 
see better progress in its implementing price hikes now (vs. limited visibility earlier in 
September). We expect NYPCB to raise ASP of BT substrates by single digits QoQ in 
4Q26 and +20% QoQ in 1Q27. This is aligned with our previous comment (Clarification on 
“no intention to hike price from 4Q26”) that we expect BT substrate prices will accelerate 
again in 2027 despite a lower-than-expected price hike in 4Q26E. We view NYPCB’s 
August results announcement as a near-term price catalyst. We have a Buy rating (1) on 
NYPCB with a 12-month TP of TWD2,460. Downside risk: weaker-than-expected demand 
for switches/PCs/ smartphones.

### Page 3

Price Target Risks and Methodology - LandMark Optoelectronics Corp.  
Valuation: We employ a discounted P/E methodology, applying a 61.5x target P/E 
multiple on our 2029E EPS, then discount it back to 2027E at a COE of 10.5% (beta 1.7x, 
risk free rate 1.6% and market risk premium at 5.1%), to derive our 12-month target 
price of NT$4,459. Our target P/E multiple is based on the same PEG&M ratio as peers in 
the photonics supply chain. We are Buy-rated on LandMark. 
Key risk: Slower-than-expected SiPh penetration; stronger-than-expected competition; 
Integrated Device Manufacturers (IDMs) offering Epitaxial (EPI) wafers in-house.

on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

Industry View Attractive
What’s Changed
UMC (2303.TW) From To
Price Target NT$138.00 NT$188.00
TSMC (2330.TW) From To
Price Target NT$2,988.00 NT$3,088.00
We think the capex budget might gradually shift to AI compute
and AI networking in 2028, as memory usage needs to be
optimized. We see positives for TSMC and UMC.
TSMC 3Q26 earnings preview: We expect the company to raise full-year revenue
guidance to the low-to-mid-40% range (vs.  above 40%) Y/Y, implying 4Q26 revenue

will rise 10%-15% Q/Q amid very strong 3nm demand. 3Q26 gross margin should
reach the high end of guidance of 67.5% However, should Intel focus on internal
capacity expansion, we think TSMC would maintain 2nm capacity expansion at the
same pace and its capex at US$54bn for 2026. For 2027, we see TSMC’s capex
reaching US$75bn to US$80bn.
In terms of 2027 wafer price hikes, we see leading edge (7nm and below) up 5%-10%
and mature nodes up 0%-3%; hence, gross margin could approach 70% in 2027. As a

result, we raise  our TSMC price target  by 3% to NT$3,088.
Key topics for upcoming TSMC 3Q26 earnings call on October 15:
1. Would TSMC consider working with TereFab  (Taiwan News, Oct 3 2026) and
building new fab sites in Texas (Bloomberg, Oct 1 2026)? Our view is that expansion
in Texas could be necessary, considering customer demand and limited space at the
Arizona fab in five years.
2. If Intel were to increase capex, would that reduce outsourcing at TSMC? Our

recent industry checks suggest some trimming of Intel’s 3nm and 2nm; how might
TSMC reallocate its resources?
3. How would TSMC address large reticle size chip demand? Our checks suggest
MediaTek handled TPU v10 IceFish reticle size initially at 14x; could TSMC’s CoWoS
or CoPoS be able to support such packaging?
We raise  our UMC price target by 36% to NT$188 on AI networking (SiPh)
business upside: Some investors remain on the sidelines amid ongoing smartphone

and PC demand weakness. We have seen some order cuts in PC semis recently, as
some consumer analog IC customers (e.g., Silergy) have not been able to pass
through UMC’s wafer price hike. Therefore, some investors do not expect any UMC’s
price hike in 2027. However, two growth drivers could help UMC raise wafer price
for 8-inch in 2027: 1) Samsung DDI outsourcing, as we noted in our earlier  Foundry
Floorplan, and 2) growing SiPh PIC demand, in particular after UMC introduced its

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
October 5, 2026 08:00 PM GMT

TSMC's 2026–28 foundry floorplan broadly aligns with our
bottom-up demand analysis
Our industry checks suggest ASML allocates capacity based largely on historical demand
and long-term partnerships, indicating that TSMC remains the largest customer for EUV
shipments. If so, TSMC should retain strong pricing power and could increase leading-edge
wafer prices by 5%–10% in 2027, reflecting both the value it delivers to customers and the
potential for margin expansion.

TSMC 2nm and 3nm capacity growth remains robust; 5nm
set to decline
Consistent with our demand forecast, TSMC indicated very strong capacity requirements
for both 2nm and 3nm starting in 2026, implying a 70% capacity CAGR for N2 from 2026

to 2028. TSMC also suggested that 5nm capacity will begin to decline from 2027, in line
with our forecast that 5nm demand will drop as Blackwell transitions to Rubin on 3nm.
Separately, advanced packaging capacity expansion should remain in focus for TSMC in
2027. This includes, but is not limited to, CoWoS and SoIC, for which we now expect
capacity to reach 200kwpm and 40kwpm, respectively, by 2027.

How might TSMC address Intel's competition in both wafer
foundry and advanced packaging?
Intel EMIB-T remains competitive in 2029; how TSMC will
address?
With demand in AI continuing to boom, we expect TSMC and its OSAT partners to
expedite capacity expansion plans for CoWoS. We see TSMC's CoWoS capacity building
further to 260kwpm in 2028, from 130/200kwpm in 2026/2027, with 2028's expansion
mainly focused in Arizona's AP9/10 and potentially in AP7. TSMC is now conducting trial

production for CoPoS in Taoyuan. If the progress goes well, we would expect TSMC to
start building its CoPoS capacity, with location also likely to be in AP9/10 or AP7P3.
We expect the non-TSMC camp to expand CoWoS capacity to 110kwpm by 2028 year-end,
from 80kwpm by end-2027. For ASE/SPIL, we expect FoCoS+CoWoS capacity to rise from
30kwpm at 2027-end to 50-60kwpm at 2028-end, with expansion focused on CoWoS-L
and CoWoS-R. For Amkor, we expect CoWoS capacity to increase from 30kwpm at 2027-

end to 55-60kwpm at 2028-end, also focused on CoWoS-L and CoWoS-R.

What about  Intel EMIB capacity build?
For Intel, we see current average EMIB-M capacity of 110kwpm falling slightly to 95kwpm
in 2027 as it converts some toward EMIB-T. We see the major customers of EMIB-M being
Trainium3, upcoming Trainium 4 and its in-house server CPU. Average EMIB-T capacity is

around 5kwpm in 2026, which we expect to further scale to 15-20kwpm in 2027 and 40-
45kwpm in 2028. All of the capacity mentioned above is converted for  12-inch wafer
equivalent, which we believe would be much more easier to communicate with compared
to TSMC CoWoS capacity.
Although some small projects are ongoing, we still see MediaTek/Google Humufish
consuming most of the capacity during 2027-28. We maintain our view that Intel EMIB-T

Indeed, we have seen some TSMC customers surveying (testing vehicles) Intel EMIB-T due
to the design for larger chip size, lower cost, and intention to seek a second source
because TSMC's CoWoS capacity is still relatively tight. However, we believe TSMC could
still try to expand its packaging size by leveraging different technologies to meet
customers' needs. We think it remains concerned about whether it is going to open its
front-end computing die production/know-how to its competitor, Intel, for back-end

packaging.
Our prediction of the trend after 2028 –  TSMC might:
1. Pull in CoPoS (panel-level process) with both 300mm and 500mm square panel
size
2. Introduce 3.5D packaging with SoIC for vertical chip stacking (Meta MTIA is
surveying)
3. Adopt glass core substrate to solve warpage for >9.7x reticle-size chips

Pros and cons of TSMC's CoWoS, Intel's EMIB, TSMC's
CoPoS, and SoIC
TSMC’s CoWoS, Intel’s EMIB, TSMC’s CoPoS, and TSMC’s SoIC represent different points
along the advanced packaging spectrum, each optimizing for a different balance of
bandwidth, cost, scalability and integration complexity. We think future design of the
chips is likely to combine these approaches—for example, using CoWoS for lateral

integration and SoIC for vertical stacking—to achieve optimal system-level performance.
Exhibit 12: Comparison between TSMC's CoWoS, CoPoS, SoIC, and Intel's EMIB-T

• CoWoS excels in high-bandwidth 2.5D integration but is costly and capacity-limited
• EMIB offers a more cost-effective and modular alternative with some performance
trade-offs
• CoPoS targets large-scale, cost-efficient integration but is still maturing
• SoIC delivers the highest performance through 3D stacking at the expense of
complexity and thermal constraints.

### Page 10

M
Idea
10
UMC is well positioned for Silicon Photonic IC (PIC) foundry
In our previous Foundry Floorplan, we presented a constructive view on UMC given its AI-
related revenue. Management indicated AI revenue of US$300mn this year (3-5% of
revenue), potentially more than tripling to US$1bn in three years (2029).
UMC has a  capacity expansion plan to capture AI demand, including a clean room for
advanced packaging for AI chips, such as wafer-on-wafer and memory-on-memory stacking.

Some investors remain on the sidelines amid ongoing smartphone and PC demand
weakness, and we have seen some order cuts of PC semis recently. Some think a UMC
price hike in 2027 wouldn’t go through, but we see two growth drivers helping it
successfully  hike wafer price for 8-inch in 2027: 1) Samsung DDI outsourcing, and 2)
growing SiPh PIC demand, in particular after UMC introduced its 400Gb per lane solution
(PIC + TFLN) solution.
How UMC is differentiated in PIC foundry

According to UMC, InP and TFLN are the two most promising solutions, as they are much
more mature than the other contenders. InP is facing severe shortage, adding to TFLN’s
momentum.
UMC Group’s TFLN program started in 2021 with 6” in mass production and 8” in
sampling. TFLN's   strengths include low loss, high bandwidth (> 110 GHz), good thermal
stability, and strong linearity.
Integrating TFLN with PIC is a challenge as well as an opportunity. Advanced packaging

schemes and micro transfer printing are both seen as promising candidates. UMC is
developing the integration technology, leveraging its expertise in PIC, TFLN, and advanced
packaging.
Adoption of higher-performance modulators (TFLN/InP) and improvement in photo diodes
are key factors affecting deployment of plain-vanilla (non-WDM, IM/DD) DCIs with 400
Gbps/channel performance and beyond, which is very close to TSMC's mainstream PIC
technology Micro Ring.

Exhibit 15: UMC differentiates in TFLN process offering for PIC
Source: UMC

About Foundry Floorplan
In 2011,  we first published this product (Asia/Pacific Foundry: The Foundry Floorplan) to
provide our analysis of foundry industry dynamics. We have resumed this practice as
global foundry competition remains active. Foundry supply is also a critical resource for
future AI chips, whether in the global market or China's local market.
Below are prior reports from this series:
• The Foundry Floorplan: All boats riding on the same tide – mature node up-cycle

begins (19 May 2026)
• The Foundry Floorplan: TSMC preview update; CoWoS vs. EMIB competition (14
Apr 2026)
• The Foundry Floorplan: Raise TSMC's 2027e and 2028e Capex (9 Mar 2026)
• The Foundry Floorplan: Update on TSMC's 2027 capex; interposer outsourcing to
Vanguard? (13 Jan 2026)
• Asia/Pacific Foundry: The Foundry Floorplan, April 2011

TSMC: Estimate and price target revisions
Our earnings forecasts increase 4% for 2026,  5% for  2027 and 3% for 2028: We raise
our 2026 revenue forecast and modestly increase our margin assumptions to reflect
stronger-than-expected 3Q26 performance, driven by robust AI demand, including
sustained Blackwell shipments. We raise our 2027–28 revenue assumptions by 3%–5%,
reflecting the increase in strong leading-edge demand as well as the wafer price hike.

Our PT increases to NT$3,088 from   NT$2,988, driven primarily by our estimate

revisions. Our PT implies a 20x P/E multiple on our new 2027 EPS forecast. TSMC
currently trades at 17x that estimate, close to its average NTM P/E of 16.5x since 2018. We
find the stock attractive at current levels.
We continue to derive our 12-month price target (base-case scenario) using a residual
income valuation framework. Our key assumptions are unchanged, including a cost of
equity of 9.2% (beta of 1.2, equity risk premium of 6.0%, and risk-free rate of 2.0%), an

intermediate growth rate of 10.5%, and a terminal growth rate of 4%.
Our bull and bear case scenario values increase to NT$3,720 (from NT$3,600) and NT
$1,700 (from NT$1,645), respectively.

UMC: Estimate and price target revisions
We raise our 2026, 2027, and 2028 earnings estimates by 56%, 25%, and 31%,
respectively: Our 2026e change is due mostly to strong 2Q26 non-op gains. We increase
2027/28e revenue and EPS due to new CPO related business contribution from 12-inch
SiPh PIC  as well as 8-inch TFLN. Our 2028e increase also includes DDI outsource from
Samsung foundry.

We raise our price target to NT$188 from NT$138: We continue to derive our price
target (base case scenario value) from a residual income model, as we believe it best
represents the value of the company. Besides our estimate increases, we raise our
intermediate growth rate from 12.5% to 13.5% due to structural changes in the industry's
supply/demand dynamics. Other key assumptions are unchanged, including a cost of

equity of 9.2% (beta of 1.2, equity risk premium of 6%, and risk-free rate of 2%),  and
terminal growth rate of 3%.
Our bull case scenario value increases from NT$200 to NT$270, and our bear case value
rises from NT$70 to  NT$95, implying 7.5x and 2.6x 2027e BVPS, respectively.

Risk Reward - UMC (2303.TW)
NT$188.00
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions: 9.2% cost of equity, 13.5% medium-term
growth, and 3.0% terminal growth.
Morgan Stanley EstimatesMean
Consensus Price Target Distribution NT$38.30 NT$258.00
Source: Reﬁnitiv, Morgan Stanley Research
MS Rating
▪ We are OW as we see structural changes
in the mature node industry's
supply/demand dynamics.
▪ Its foundry quality is comparable to
TSMC's in mature nodes. Along with

specialty technologies, this could help offset
part of the margin downside.
▪ Non-AI market recovery remains uncertain
in 2027, yet UMC should still beneﬁt from
strong AI-related demand spillover from its
foundry peers.
▪ 12nm FinFET development is strategic. It
should be one of UMC's long-term
strengths, notably when Chinese foundries
cannot effectively expand FinFET capacity.
▪ The stock now trades at 4.1x 2027e BVPS.
That is well above its historical average

since 2009, but our price target implies 5.1x.

View descriptions of Risk Rewards Themes here
NT$270.00
7.5x 2027e BVPS
Stabilized semi demand; better-than-
expected 8-inch and trailing-edge 12-inch
demand: 1) Global semi sales grow >15% Y/Y

in 2026, and ex-TSMC foundry industry
grows >10% Y/Y; 2) UMC gains share in both
8- and 12-inch wafers thanks to technology
differentiation; 3) UMC receives more
outsourcing from customers, which helps ﬁll
22/28nm capacity and lifts blended wafer
ASP; 4) traction increases for UMC's
12nm/14nm FinFET technologies.
NT$188.00
5.2x 2027e BVPS
Healthy semi growth; improving demand for
8-inch and trailing-edge 12-inch: 1) Global
semi sales grow ~15% in 2026 and ex-TSMC

foundry industry grows 3-5% Y/Y; 2) 8-inch
utilization rate is above 75% with steady 12-
inch demand amid semi inventory digestion;
3) UMC hikes wafer prices slightly in
response to strong AI-related demand.
NT$95.00
2.6x 2027e BVPS
Signiﬁcant semi industry decline; 8-inch and
trailing-edge 12-inch face weakening
demand: 1) Global semi sales grow only <5%
in 2026; 2) display semi and power semi
demand deteriorates further; 3) signiﬁcant
wafer pricing erosion given low fab

utilization and mature node peers'
competition; 4) depreciation ramp-up of
more than 30% Y/Y in 2026 without
sufﬁcient wafer order inﬂow to sustain
UMC's gross margin.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

WinWay Technology
Valuation
We value WinWay using a PE-based approach that factors in earnings momentum and likely expansion of its addressable
market. Our target price of NT$11,500 is derived from applying c.41x to 2H27E-1H28E EPS, higher than the upper end of its
long-term PE range, which we believe is justified given the company's entrenched position in the high-end AI GPU testing
supply chain, rising contribution from coaxial socket and SLT solutions, and the increasing value of test content per module.

With these structural drivers in place, and as we believe the market is starting to value it as an AI/HPC play (rather than a
consumer electronics stock), we expect the multiple to hold at a premium relative to its historical averages.
Risks
Our quant rating system rates the WinWay stock High Risk based on its historical volatility.
Key downside risks that could impede the stock from reaching our target price and our earnings forecasts include: 1) client and

sector concentration – specifically any delays in US AI GPU product roadmap could impact the business outlook for WinWay; 2)
intensifying competition in coaxial and hypersockets from global peers; 3) faster-than-expected testing algorithm
optimization, which could shorten testing durations and reduce the anticipated hardware demand multiplier; and 4) delay of
MEMS Phase 2 mass production.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

## Document 080: 261006_ml_kingslide

Source PDF: all report pdf/261006_ml_kingslide.pdf
Report source: 未公開來源

### Page 1

King Slide

Maintain Buy: Sep sales -31% MoM from 
high bases; Aug earnings remain solid

Maintain Rating: BUY | PO: 14,888 TWD | Price: 13,100 TWD

Sep sales -31% MoM from high bases; 3Q26 sales in-line 
King Slide’s Sep sales of NT$4.46bn declined 31% MoM (+196% YoY), after the strong 
sales in the past few months (up 17-46% MoM in Apr-Jul and up 1% MoM in Aug). 3Q26 
sales of NT$17.34bn (+60% QoQ, +297% YoY) were 0.5% above BofAe of NT$17.26bn 
and 0.9% below consensus of NT$17.49bn.  
We are not surprised with the MoM sales drop in Sep as (1) the company’s MoM sales 
trend has been volatile (Exhibit 1 and Exhibit 2), (2) sales bases in Apr-Aug were at 
record levels, (3) contribution from Nvidia Rubin GPU projects should be more 
meaningful in 4Q26 after hardware components ramp-up, and (4) partial general server 
orders/demand likely were pushed out due to key component shortage (e.g. memory, 
CPU). 
Solid Aug EPS NT$40.07; pre-tax/net margin 74.6%/59.1% 
In addition to Sep sales, on the same day (6 Oct), King Slide also announced its Aug 
unaudited key financial data as required by local regulations (Exhibit 3). Aug sales were 
NT$6,466mn (+344% YoY), while pre-tax profit was NT$4,826mn (+216% YoY) and net 
income was NT$3,819mn (+212% YoY). EPS came in at NT$40.07 (+212% YoY). Pre-tax 
margin and net margin were 74.6% and 59.1%, respectively.  
Compared to July unaudited figures announced on 14 Aug (see our 17 Aug report), 
including sales of NT$6,407mn, pre-tax margin of 93.4%, net margin of 74.7%, and EPS 
of NT$50.24, we believe the margin and EPS differences mainly reflected FX impact 
under similar sales scale. 
Combining Jul and Aug unaudited results, sales totaled NT$12.87bn and EPS reached 
NT$90.31. Pre-tax margin and net margin were 84.0% and 66.9%, respectively. Jul + Aug 
sales achieved 75%/74% of BofAe/consensus 3Q26 sales. Pre-tax profit, net income, 
and EPS all achieved 79% of BofAe (79.4% pre-tax margin, 62.9% net margin, and 
NT$113.87 EPS) and 77-78% of consensus (80.3% pre-tax margin, 63.1% net margin, 
and NT$117.62 EPS). We also noted that Jul + Aug EPS of NT$90.31 already surpassed 
EPS of NT$74.38 in 2Q26 with similar pre-tax/net margin (84.0%/66.9% in Jul + Aug, vs. 
84.2%/65.4% in 2Q26). 
Maintain Buy; expect a neutral-to-positive market reaction 
We expect a neutral-to-positive market reaction in the near term in view of significant 
MoM sales decline in Sep but also still solid Aug unaudited data. We maintain Buy on 
King Slide as the server rail market leader with broad and diversified client/project base. 
We expect the company to post 3Q26 results after market close on 12 Nov (Thu).

Price objective basis & risk 
King Slide (KGSLF) 
Our PO of NT$14,888 is based on 32x 2027E EPS. We use 32x P/E multiple (2.5SD 
above P/E average since listing) in view of strong AI server market demand, leading 
position in server rail, solid/high margin profiles, and double-digit sales/EPS CAGR in 
2026-28E. Its early step into server market since 2001 with proven track record, much 
larger scale than peer, and 3,600+ patents as strong moats for high/above-peer margins. 
Its above-30x P/E is a similar case to other component sub-sector leaders in Taiwan.

Upside risks to our PO are: (1) even stronger AI server demand, (2) margin upside thanks 
to larger scale and/or better efficiency, (3) better kitchen slides market demand, (4) 
faster capacity expansion, (5) favorable FX movement (mainly USD/NTD) for GM and/or 
non-operating items.

Downside risks to our PO are: (1) softer AI server demand due to GPU/components 
shortage, (2) GM pressure due to higher raw material cost (e.g. steel, aluminum), (3) 
softer kitchen slides market demand under macro uncertainty/weakness, (4) delayed 
product certification, (5) slower capacity expansion, (6) unfavorable FX movement 
(mainly USD/NTD) to weigh on GM and/or non-operating items.

Hiwin Technologies Corp.  | Asia Pacific
September Sales Flat MoM/
+30% YoY
In this report, we focus on Hiwin's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for its share price.
Details:
• September sales were NT$2,720mn (flat MoM/+30% YoY).
• 3Q26 revenue came in at NT$8,110mn (+8% QoQ/+35% YoY), largely in line
with our estimate at NT$7,996mn (+6% QoQ/+33% YoY) and the  consensus
of NT$7,964mn (+6% QoQ/+33% YoY).
Our view:
• On top of the sequential growth on the top line, we believe that Hiwin

should also further expand its gross margin on a QoQ basis in 3Q26, owing
to the higher utilization and the tailwinds of price hikes.
• We think the momentum should remain favorable in the near term.
• We stay constructive on the stock, with current valuation at 29x 2027e P/E
vs. a peak cycle level of 35-40x.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Hiwin Technologies Corp. (2049.TW)
Base case, P/E. We think this valuation methodology better captures Hiwin's earnings growth
momentum, as its valuation tends to move with the business cycle. We apply a target multiple
of 38x to our 2027 EPS estimate. We view this multiple as justified by the 47% operating profit
CAGR we project for 2025-28.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger industrial automation demand pickup.

n Market share gains.
n Earlier-than-expected revenue contribution from humanoids.
Risks to Downside
n Weaker industrial automation demand.
n More pricing pressure.
n Market share loss.
n More broad-based impact on the global economy from geopolitical risks.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

漲價紅利延續；特殊材料蓄勢待發 
 漲價不斷，2H26 獲利將持續跳升：2H26 營運動能持續增溫，主要
受惠 CCL 缺貨，不斷漲價，獲利預期出現跳升。M6 以下、無鉛、
無鹵素及高 TG 等一般材料訂單外溢至騰輝，騰輝 2026 年一般材料
價格 YTD+50%，一般材料已貢獻公司獲利超過一半，9 月份一般材
料再續漲 10~15%；且一般材料產品組合持續轉佳，8 月份起單價與
獲利率較佳之高 TG 產品出貨量成長超過一倍 ， 基本上目前公司所有
產品線毛利率皆達 30%以上，至於特殊材料仍穩定貢獻。 
 深化特殊材料布局 ， 期待新專案驅動中長線成長：截至 3Q26 VT-901
產品營收+70~80%YOY，除了軍工需求倍增，亦有數家國際半導體
設備大廠將 VT-901 用於半導體老化測試板。近期騰輝新增 8～9 個
散熱專案，拓展至 AI 與高功率電源散熱應用，提供後續特殊材料成
長動能。高速材料方面，騰輝亦有送樣下一世代 AI 專案(碳氫 CCL
與 PTFE 皆有送樣)，期待成為 AI 終端客戶二供廠商。 
 買進評等：預期一般材料漲價效益可延續至 2027 年，且若新專案驗
證順利，有望進一步推升產品組合。預估 2026/2027 年 EPS 分別為
15.12/30.86 元，評等買進，目標價 460 元(15x2027EPS)。

個股報告 
騰輝電子-KY 產品主要分一般材
料與特殊材料

騰輝電子-KY 主要從事印刷電路板所需之黏合片、銅箔 基板及鋁基板生產與銷
售，一般材料產品應用於光電板、汽車板等中低階產品，特殊材料主要為散熱
鋁基板，另外亦有著墨軍工航天等利基型領域， 2025 年產品組合：一般材料
41%、特殊材料 53%、其他 6%；2026 年一般材料受惠漲價、缺貨，預估2026
年產品組合：一般材料 53%、特殊材料 41%、其他 6%。騰輝總部位於中國蘇
州，主要生產基地位亦位於中國蘇州(月產能 80 萬張)、江陰(月產能 20 萬張)、
深圳(月產能 10 萬張)與台灣(月產能 10 萬張)， 泰國廠(月產能 10 萬張)則於 2026
年 9 月開始量產。

Sources: Company、Masterlink

漲價不斷，2H26 獲利將持續跳升  騰輝 2H26 營運動能持續增溫，主要受惠 CCL 缺貨，不斷漲價，獲利預期出現
跳升。CCL 上游原料方面，E 布維持逐月漲價，而銅箔供給緊張程度僅次於玻
纖布；CCL 方面，CCL 龍頭廠商將產能集中於 M6 以上高速材料，中低階 CCL
供給明顯收縮，M6 以下、無鉛、無鹵素及高 TG 等一般材料訂單外溢至騰輝，
騰輝 2026 年一般材料價格 YTD+50%，一般材料已貢獻公司獲利超過一半，9
月份一般材料再續漲 10~15%；且一般材料產品組合持續轉佳，8 月份起單價與
獲利率較佳之高 TG 產品出貨量成長超過一倍 ， 基本上目前公司所有產品線毛利
率皆達 30%以上，至於特殊材料仍穩定貢獻；綜上所述，預估3Q26/4Q26 毛利
率將分別提升至 39.8%/41.6%， 推估3Q26/4Q26 EPS 分別為 4.38 元/6.04 元，
合計推估 2026 全年 EPS 為 15.12 元。

深化特殊材料布局，期待新專案
驅動中長線成長 
 騰輝長期聚焦利基型特殊材料市場，並具備自行合成樹脂能力，除了掌握關鍵
配 方 外 ， 亦 可 降 低 原 料 成 本 。PI 方 面 ， 截 至 3Q26 V T-901 產 品 營 收
+70~80%YOY，除了軍工需求倍增，亦有數家國際半導體設備大廠將 VT-901
用於半導體老化測試板，PI 產品毛利率約有 70%高水準；其他高毛利率產品如
不流膠 PP 營收亦有好表現，截至 3Q26 不流膠 PP 營收+50%YOY。另外，騰
輝為全球主要車燈散熱材料供應商，其散熱鋁基板 80%應用於汽車照明，近期
騰輝新增 8～9 個散熱專案，拓展至 AI 與高功率電源散熱應用，提供後續特殊
材料成長動能。高速材料方面，騰輝亦有送樣下一世代 AI 專案(碳氫 CCL 與
PTFE 皆有送樣)，期待成為 AI 終端客戶二供廠商。產能方面，目前稼動率水準
未達滿載以提供訂單彈性；泰國新廠今年 9 月開始試加工，初期由裁切等後段
製程開始，上膠機預計 1H27 進廠裝機，完整產線預計 2027 年到位，目前規劃
至少兩條產線，若全產能運行，預估可增加 10～15%產能，除支應既有客戶需
求外，亦為 2027 年新產品放量預留成長空間；此外，騰輝亦通過 15 億元昆山
擴建案，新產能亦預計於 2027 年展開貢獻。

維持買進評等

騰輝受惠漲價、轉單及產品組合優化，帶動營收與毛利率同步提升；考量 上游
原料與 CCL 供給短期仍難明顯改善 ， 預期一般材料漲價效益可延續至2027 年。
至於特殊材料方面，預期 2027 年 PI、不流膠 PP 等產品持續成長，另外騰輝積

買進，目標價 460 元(15x2027EPS)。

BVPS(NT$) 77.1

進入傳統旺季且 2027 年動能可期 
 近期營運：時序將進入傳統旺季，預估九月營收跳增，4Q26 進入旺
季，預估出貨量 QoQ 與 YoY 雙增，預估 4Q26 營收 54.4 億元，
+18.77% QoQ，+9.91% YoY，營益率隨稼動率提升且有規模提升所
帶來的營運槓桿效益提升，預估 EPS 1.51 元；2026 年 EPS 5.02 元，
以過往配息率 70%計算，推估現金股利 3.5 元，殖利率超過 5.5%。 
 未來展望：2026 年 ADIDAS 持平、NIKE 仍在調整中、Brooks 穩
定、Asics 持續面臨越南場招工不易影響出貨，預估 2026 年出貨量
約 3700~3800 萬雙。2027 年隨著印尼新廠放量且越南新廠完工 ， 產
能效益有望在 2H27 顯現，且搭配奧運前一年拉貨效應，2027 年有
望恢復增長態勢，我們預估 2027 年出貨量 4200~4300 萬雙。 
 產能效益在 2027 年顯現：印尼廠已於 2H25 開線，惟目前單月出
貨量仍未顯著提升。印尼一期目標年產能為 600 萬雙，未來將陸續
新增非美客戶投入產線，屆時印尼廠出貨增溫。越南新廠方面，目前
已有一棟廠房完工，預計 1Q27 試產，2H27 放量，屆時 2027 年印
尼與越南產能投放效益顯現，將為集團帶來新的營運動能。 
 2027 年在 ADIDAS 穩健成長、NIKE 持平；Brooks、Asics 出貨持續
放量，預估出貨量雙位數成長達 4300~4400 萬雙，不考慮匯率波動
影響下 ， 預估2027 年營收 229.76 億元(+10.43% YoY)， 隨著稼動率
提升且新廠效益逐步顯現 ， 毛利率有望提升至 18~19%間， 預估營業
利益 17.25 億元，營業利益率 7.51%，預估 EPS 7.06 元。 
 調升評等至買進 ：預估 2026 年 EPS 5.02 元、2027 年 EPS 7.06 元。
考量營運谷底已過，長期投資買浮現，且時序即將進入旺季、 2027
年營運恢復增長，股價下檔風險有限，故調升評等至買進，目標價
98 元。

志強為全球足球鞋代工領導廠
商，全球市占率 20%以上

志強為全球足球鞋代工領導廠商，全球市占率 20%以上 
志強-KY 於 2000 年開始進入運動鞋代工領域，以營收佔比來看，2024 年運動
鞋佔 84.81%、休閒鞋 13.8%以及其他 1.39%。以生產基地來看，越南為主要生
產基地，佔出貨比重約2/3、其次為柬埔寨，共有六個廠區，分別為志雄(越南)、
志中(越南)、志寧(越南)、大華(越南)、大河(越南)以及志寨(柬埔寨)，合計年產
能約 4500 萬雙。志強-KY 四大品牌客戶為 Nike、Adidas、Brooks 以及 Asics，
1H26 ADIDAS 佔營收比重約 60.5~61%、NIKE 24~25%、Brooks 7~7.5%以及
ASICS 5.5~6%。2023 年受到客戶庫存調整影響 ， 出貨量降至3039 萬雙 ，2024
年出貨量達 3471 萬雙，YOY+14.21%，2025 年出貨量接近 3950 萬雙，2026
年受到全球消費景氣不明朗且主要客戶下單趨於謹慎影響，預估出貨量衰退 ，
預估 2026 年出貨量約 3700~3800 萬雙。

志強現有生產基地

Source ：Company Data 、Masterlink

時序即將進入旺季，九月營收跳
增，預估 3Q26 EPS 0.8 4 元；
4Q26 營運向上，預估 2026 年
EPS 5.02 元

What’s New： 
時序即將進入旺季，九月營收跳增，預估3Q26 EPS 0.84 元；4Q26 營運向上，
預估 2026 年 EPS 5.02 元 
2Q26 受到印尼以及葡萄牙兩個新廠開始出貨 ， 初期仍在負毛利階段且印尼有長
假效應影響 ，2Q26 毛利率 17%， 營業利益率5.7%， 稅後歸屬母公司淨利潤2.93
億元，EPS 1.47 元，1H26 EPS 2.67 元。3Q26 進入傳統製鞋淡季，預期出貨
量 QOQ 下滑 ，YOY 小增， 預估營收45.80 億元，-15.63% QoQ，+10.54% YoY，
營益率受淡季影響，預估小幅向下，預估 EPS 0.84 元。然就月趨勢來看，時序
即將進入傳統旺季，預估九月營收 將跳增，4Q26 進入傳統旺季，預估出貨量
QoQ 與 YoY 雙增，我們預估 4Q26 營收 54.4 億元，+18.77% QoQ ，+9.91% 
YoY，營益率隨稼動率提升且有規模提升所帶來的營運槓桿效益， 預估本業獲利
3.48 億元，營業利益率 6.4%，以現行股本 20.01 億元計，EPS 1.51 元；預估
2026 年 EPS 5.02 元，以過往配息率 70%計算，推估現金股利 3.5 元，殖利率
超過 5.5%。

出貨量(千)

時序即將進入旺季，出貨動能增
溫，2027 年出貨恢復增長態勢 
 時序即將進入旺季，出貨動能增溫，2027 年出貨恢復增長態勢 
志強-KY 年產量約 4000~4300 萬雙，2019 年集團出貨量為 4100 萬雙，以足球
鞋為主力，2019 年足球鞋出貨量 2100 萬雙，全球市占率 21.03%，2019 年佔
Nike 足球鞋 35%、佔 Adidas 足球鞋的 38%。2020 年受到疫情影響，出貨量降
至 3300 萬雙 ，YoY 衰退 24%， 其中運動鞋衰退28%、 休閒鞋小幅衰退2.74%。
2021 年受到越南疫情導致停工衝擊，2021 年出貨量約 3344 萬雙，與 2020 年
約略持平。2022 年，在疫情衝擊下供應商大者仍大的趨勢更加明顯，上半年品
牌對於產能的需求殷切，終端需求面隨著歐美全面開放品牌端需求持續暢旺，
爾後 4Q22 開始進入庫存調整，2022 年全年出貨量為 4269 萬雙。2023 年品牌
客戶持續庫存調整，2023 年出貨量約 3039 萬雙。2024 年隨著客戶庫存調整完
畢且通路庫存持續去化，出貨量 3471 萬雙。2025 年受到關稅因素干擾，品牌
對於 2H25 下單趨於謹慎，2025 年出貨量約 3950 萬雙，其中 ADIDAS 維持
雙 位 數 成 長 、NIKE 趨 於 保 守 且 Brooks 及 Asics 出 貨 高 成 長 。2026 年
ADIDAS 持平、NIKE 仍在調整中，預估中~高個位數衰退；Brooks 穩定、Asics 
持續面臨越南場招工不 易影響，導致出貨量無法顯著放量，預估 志強全年出貨
量中個位數衰退 ， 就趨勢上來看 ，1Q26 與 2Q26 均維持在 940~950 萬雙水準，
1H26 出貨量 1890 萬雙 、2H26 YOY 持平到小增， 預估全年出貨量約3700~3800
萬雙。2027 年隨著印尼新廠放量且越南新廠完工 ， 產能效益有望在2H27 顯現，
且搭配奧運前一年拉貨效應，2027 年有望恢復增長態勢，我們預估 2027 年出
貨量 4200~4300 萬雙，其中 ADIDAS 穩健、NIKE 持平；Brooks、Asics 出貨
持續放量。

預估 2026 年 EPS 為 5.02 元，2027 年 EPS 為 7.06 元 
2026 年持續受到關稅且全球消費景氣不明朗， 品牌下單持續謹慎 ，其中 ADIDAS
穩定、NIKE 持續調整且 Brooks 及 Asics 高成長，我們預期出貨量 3700~3800
萬雙，且受到新廠稼動影響 ，2026 年獲利呈現衰退 ， 惟最壞時刻已過 ， 預估2026
年 EPS 5.02 元；2027 年在 ADIDAS 穩健成長、NIKE 持平；Brooks、Asics 出
貨持續放量，預估出貨量雙位數成長達 4300~4400 萬雙，不考慮匯率波動影響
下，預估 2027 年營收 229.76 億元，+10.43% YoY，隨著稼動率提升且新廠效
益逐步顯現，毛利率有望提升至 18~19%間，預估營業利益 17.25 億元，營業
利益率 7.51%，預估稅後歸屬母公司淨利潤 14.14 億元，EPS 7.06 元。

治療藥物將取證，AB-BNCT 邁向商業化階段 
 B10 L-BPA 治療藥物 2026 年底取得台灣藥證 ：禾榮科 AB-BNCT 設
備已於 2024 年 7 月正式取得 TFDA 醫療器材許可證 ， 其核心治療新
藥 B10 L -BPA 之復發型頭頸癌已完成收案，主要評估指標為治療後
三個月的客觀反應率（ORR） ，預計2026 年底取得藥證，取得藥證
後 ， 將可正式收治病患 ， 且未來將擴大適應症至三陰性乳癌與肺腺癌 。 
 1H27 年北榮 BNCT 機台將完成驗收：禾榮科積極布局國內外市場，
在國內已與中國醫藥大學新竹附設醫院展開產學合作， 2024 年 11
月正式啟動臨床收案；臺北榮民總醫院的建置工程則於 2025 年 8 月
動工，預計 1Q27 完工，1H27 可認列新台幣 12 億元營收；海外方
面，佳世達集團旗下的南京明基醫院斥資 5500 萬美元採購設備，同
時禾榮科技攜手新加坡國立大學醫院共建研發樞紐 ， 成功將台灣自主
研發的 AB-BNCT 癌症治療技術推向國際舞台。 
 禾榮科治療藥物即將取得許可證，屆時即可正式開始收費，同時，
1H27 將認列台北榮總 BNCT 設備 12 億元營收 ，2027 年開始轉虧為
盈，未來隨著適應症擴展及海外布局，營收與獲利將爆發成長，禾榮
科投資評等維持買進，預估 2027 年 EPS 6.29 元，目標價 700 元。

個股報告 
禾 榮 科 核 心 技 術 為 自 主 研 發
的 AB -BNCT 癌症治療設備  
 禾榮科成立於 2017 年，專注於發展先進的癌症治療技術，公司核心業務為加
速器型硼中子捕獲治療（Accelerator-Based Boron Neut ron Capture Therapy, 
AB-BNCT），此項技術源自清華大學與工業技術研究院歷經三十年的研究成
果，禾榮科成功將這項學研基礎轉化為商業應用，提供完整的 AB-BNCT 整體
解決方案，涵蓋質子加速器系統、中子源靶系統、中子束調整器、治療控制系
統、治療計畫系統以及病人定位系統等關鍵設備，致力於為醫療機構與患者帶
來精準、高效的治療選擇。

B10 L -BPA 治 療 藥 物 預 計
2026 年底取得台灣藥證  
 禾榮科技 AB-BNCT 設備已於 2024 年 7 月正式取得 TFDA 醫療器材許可證，其
核心治療新藥 B10 L-BPA (10B-L-Boronophenylalanine)正進行復發型頭頸癌、
惡性腦瘤及復發型腦膜瘤的臨床試驗，復發型頭頸癌收案 24 人，主要評估指標
為治療後三個月的客觀反應率 （ORR） ，預計2026 年底取得藥證，目前禾榮科
營收仍主要來自恩慈治療，取得藥證後，將可正式收治病患，且未來將擴大適
應症至三陰性乳癌與肺腺癌等；此外，搭配術前精準評估劑量的 F18 FBPA 診
斷用藥已於 4Q25 進入臨床試驗。

Exhibit : 禾榮科 取證進度

1H27 年北榮 BNCT 機台將
完 成 驗 收 ， 認 列 新 台 幣12
億元營收  
 禾榮科技採取「國內樹立標竿、國外商轉輸出」的雙軌策略，積極布局全球市
場，在國內已與中國醫藥大學新竹附設醫院展開產學合作，於 2024 年 11 月正
式啟動臨床收案；臺北榮民總醫院的建置工程則於 2025 年 8 月動工，預計
1Q27 完工，1H27 可認列新台幣 12 億元營收；此外，公司亦新簽署義大醫院與
竹銘醫院合作意向，持續拓展服務版圖。

海外方面，佳世達集團旗下的南京明基醫院斥資 5500 萬美元採購設備，同時
禾榮科技攜手新加坡國立 大學醫院共建研發樞紐，成功將台灣自主研發的
AB-BNCT 癌症治療技術推向國際舞台。

AB-BNCT合作案 合作性質 / 模式 建置與臨床進度
中國醫藥大學新竹 產學醫共同投資，中醫大出地，禾榮科出設備 2024年5月落成，同年11月啟動頭頸癌臨床試驗收案
臺北榮民總醫院 漢民創辦人黃民奇捐贈12億設備至北榮，委由禾榮科技於北榮建置 2025年8月動工，預計2027年完工
南京明基醫院 明基斥資5500萬美元向禾榮科採購設備 2025年8月正式簽約，現正進行中心場地建置規劃
新加坡國立大學醫院 共建東南亞首座 AB-BNCT 研發與臨床樞紐 2025年12月正式簽署合作協議。
竹銘醫院 整合產學研醫資源，共同規劃「硼中子捕獲治療暨前瞻影像中心」 2026年1月7日正式簽署意向書
義大醫院 雙方將針對臨床應用、特色醫療、治療中心規劃與未來合作模式進一步評估 2026年6月15日正式簽署意向書
B dot Medical 共同開拓日本及亞洲醫療市場 2026年6月25日正式簽署意向書

Source: Company Data、Masterlink

禾 榮 科 獲 利 來 自 「 設 備 銷
售 、 設 備 維 保 、 治 療 藥 物 、
診斷藥物」四大支柱  
 在設備銷售方面，禾榮科技提供買斷、租賃與分潤等多元商業模式， AB-BNCT
系統單台售價約為新台幣 12 至 16 億元，設備售出後每年可穩定收取設備售價
5%至 9%的維修保養費用，若採分潤模式，BNCT 治療費用為 60 萬元，加藥費
為 100 萬元，禾榮科可抽取治療費用的 30%至 40%作為收益。

在藥物佈局上，核心治療藥物 BPA 平均每人次收費為新台幣 40 萬元，目前於
臨床試驗與恩慈治療中使用，待取得藥證後即可獨立進行商業化銷售；而診斷
藥物 FBPA 每次費用約新台幣 4 至 8 萬元，且其用量遠大於治療藥物。

2027 年 開 始 收 治病患，且
認 列 一 台 設 備 ， 預 估EPS 
6.29 元  
 展望 2027 年，預估營收 23 億元，中醫大 1Q27 開始貢獻營收，北榮 2Q27 開
始貢獻營收，預估 2027 年藥費與治療分潤貢獻營收 11 億元，設備銷售認列營
收 12 億元；藥費與分潤毛利率超過 80%，設備銷售毛利率超過 50%，預估
2027 年毛利率 65%，預估 2027 年 EPS 6.29 元。

維持 買進評等 ，目標價 700
元  
 禾榮科治療藥物即將取得許可證，屆時即可正式開始收費，同時， 1H27 將認
列台北榮總 BNCT 設備 12 億元營收，2027 年開始轉虧為盈，未來隨著適應症
擴展及海外布局，營收與獲利將爆發成長，禾榮科投資評等維持買進，目標價
700 元。

### Page 1

NYPCB

Alarm cleared with new drivers into 2027; 
re-it. Buy with PO raise to NT$2,000

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 2,000 TWD | Price: 1,340 TWD

BT substrate price hike resumed from 4Q26 
Following (see report) the worries of moderating BT substrate price hike in early 
September, we now expect NYPCB to still raise relevant ASP by single-digit % in 4Q26, 
and the hike is purely driven by favorable S/D which will fully translate into the accretion 
in bottom-line. Following this, BT substrate price will likely be raised by another 20+% in 
1Q27, due partly to the cost pass-through. We currently bake in ~10% revenue QoQ with 
32-33% GM for 4Q26, but we do not rule out the possibility for its revenue to surprise 
on the upside, should there be a faster ramp of its ABF substrate capacity in Kunshan. 
New driver for 2027: server CPU & mix optimization  
Looking into 2027 we expect the benefits of demand overflow on (see report) server 
CPU side to materialize, and we flag a chance for the firm to record 20+% revenue from 
the emerging business with AMD server CPU, where NYPCB is working on the 
qualification in its Shulin site currently and should capture most of the spillover demand 
if the progress goes smooth. Meanwhile, the firm will likely allocate more capacity from 
other sites (e.g. Kunshan) to fulfill the demand from Broadcom, which also helps enhance 
the mix. Currently we model ~50% GM for its ABF substrate business in 2027, but we 
see chance for further upward revision, driven by more favorable S/D dynamics and mix.  
Raise estimates/PO; prime beneficiary of price hike 
NYPCB’s share price (up ~50%) lags its major peers Unimicron/Kinsus (up ~70%) since 
late-July, but in our view it is still the best proxy to position for price hike in the 
substrate market. Judging from (see report) Kinsus’ August prints last week, we believe 
NYPCB remains on track to meet or beat the market’s expectations on N-T margins, and 
we reiterate Buy on the stock. Along with 13%/18% EPS raise in 2027/28E thanks to the 
more benign pricing/margin outlook driven by further mix improvement and S/D, we 
raise PO to NT$2,000 (from NT$1,700) same based on 35x 2H27-1H28E P/E.

Company Sector 
Semiconductors

Company Description 
Founded in 1997, NYPCB was listed on the Taiwan Stock 
Exchange in 2006. The firm is a 67%-owned subsidiary of 
Nan Ya Plastics Corporation (1303 TT). NYPCB's main 
products are HDI, PCB and IC substrates (including ABF and 
BT substrates), widely used in telecommunication, 
consumers, PC, and Auto applications. The clients include 
AMD, Broadcom, Samsung, Apple, Continental, etc. The 
main competitors include Unimicron, Kinsus, Ibiden, 
Shinko, and SEMCO, etc.

Investment Rationale 
We have a Buy rating on NYPCB, in view of its continuous 
improvement of utilization rate and gross margin across 
ABF and BT substrate business into 2027, thanks to the 
robust demand growth from fabless customers and 
demand overflow in AI ASICs.

### Page 5

Price objective basis & risk 
NYPCB (NANYF) 
Our PO of NT$2,000 is based on 35x 2H27-1H28E P/E. We view this multiple as fair 
given ROE and operating margin expansion to 39% and 41% in 2027-28E (vs 13% and 
14% in 2025-26E).

Upside risks to our PO are (1) tighter ABF supply, (2) higher SiP/AiP adoption, and (3) 
less intensified competition pressure in PCB business.

Downside risks are (1) greater ABF expansion from peers, (2) muted SiP/AiP adoption, 
and (3) more severe competition in PCB business.

iQmethod are: A consistently structured, detailed, and transparent methodology. Guidelines to maximize the effectiveness of the comparative valuation process, and to identify some common pitfalls. 
iQdatabase ®  is our real-time global research database that is sourced directly from our equity analysts’ earnings models and includes forecasted as well as historical data for income statements, balance sheets, and cash

FUNDAMENTAL EQUITY OPINION KEY: Opinions include a Volatility Risk Rating, an Investment Rating and an Income Rating. VOLATILITY RISK RATINGS, indicators 
of potential price fluctuation, are: A - Low, B - Medium and C - High. INVESTMENT RATINGS reflect the analyst’s assessment of both a stock’s absolute total return 
potential as well as its attractiveness for investment relative to other stocks within its Coverage Cluster (defined below). Our investment rating s are: 1 - Buy stocks 
are expected to have a total return of at least 10% and are the most attractive stocks in the coverage cluster; 2 - Neutral stocks are expected to remain flat or 
increase in value and are less attractive than Buy rated stocks and 3 - Underperform stocks are the least attractive stocks in a coverage cluster. An investment 
rating of 6 (No Rating) indicates that a stock is no longer trading on the basis of fundamentals. Analysts assign investment ratings considering, among other things, 
the 0-12 month total return expectation for a stock and the firm’s guidelines for ratings dispersions (shown in the table below). The current price objective for a 
stock should be referenced to better understand the total return expectation at any given time. The price objective reflects the analyst’s view of the potential price 
appreciation (depreciation). 
Investment rating Total return expectation (within 12-month period of date of initial 
rating) 
Ratings dispersion guidelines for coverage clusterR2 
Buy ≥ 10% ≤ 70% 
Neutral ≥ 0% ≤ 30% 
Underperform N/A ≥ 20% 
R2Ratings dispersions may vary from time to time where BofA Global Research believes it better reflects the investment prospects of stocks in a Coverage Cluster. 
INCOME RATINGS, indicators of potential cash dividends, are: 7 - same/higher (dividend considered to be secure), 8 - same/lower (dividend not considered to be 
secure) and 9 - pays no cash dividend.  Coverage Cluster is comprised of stocks covered by a single analyst or two  or more analysts sharing a common industry, 
sector, region or other classification(s). A stock’ s coverage cluster is included in the most recent BofA Global Research report referencing the stock.

### Page 3

3【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
結論一：未來三年矽晶圓受AI帶動，需求成長遠高於供給成長
資料來源：SEMI、福邦投顧預估
• 議價權已轉回上游。2026/7美光與環球晶簽5億美元、10年期策略長約；客戶庫存去化進入尾聲，LTA循
環位置在復甦中段。
• 供給端受五大廠寡占＋擴產紀律約束，年增率長期僅1~3%（僅2017-18、2021擴產潮達6~8%）；需求端
2023-24連兩年衰退後，2025年+5.8%回升，2026-28年在AI帶動下估連三年+9~12%，供給無彈性遇上需求
高成長，是本輪缺口的核心結構。
• 價格與供需同步驗證：12吋ASP年均自2025年63美元落底，隨缺口擴大2027/2028年估達101/172美元。

4【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
結論二：預期矽晶圓2027年將供不應求，價格持續向上
資料來源：SEMI、各公司法說會，福邦投顧整理
• 量的復甦：SEMI統計2025年全球矽晶圓出貨面積12,973 MSI（+5.8%）終結連兩年衰退；2Q26出貨3,573 MSI
、年增7.4%、季增9.1%，創14季新高；SEMI預估2028年達15,485 MSI改寫歷史新高。需求增速遠高於供給放
量進程，研究員預估2027下半年正式轉為供不應求，2028年缺口約8%。
• 價格已經落地：2026/7信越、SUMCO、環球晶三大廠同步發出漲價函，12吋常規邏輯晶圓漲5~8%、AI/HPC
高階晶圓漲18~22%；信越7月法說指各家12吋已滿載、產業進入產能受限狀態，新一輪LTA將反映成本上漲，
價格上行趨勢確立。

### Page 5

矽晶圓為寡占、高壁壘的上游材料

### Page 6

6【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
矽晶圓位居半導體最上游，台灣連續16年為最大材料市場
資料來源：SEMI、TrendForce，福邦投顧整理
• 矽晶圓是所有晶片製造的起點與地基，約占晶圓製造材料22.9%、居各類材料之首，為需求量最大的半導體材料。台灣是全
球材料重心：2025年台灣半導體材料市場217億美元、占全球732億美元約29%，連續16年全球之冠（SEMI，2026）；
SUMCO銷售額34.7%來自台灣。台灣在中游（環球晶全球第三）與下游（台積電代工市占72.3%，1Q26創新高）均居要角，
上下游完整。
上游:原料與設備 中游:晶圓製造 下游:應用與代工
72.3%

### Page 7

7【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
五道工序中拉晶定生死，資本與技術雙密集
資料來源：國信證券、國泰海通，福邦投顧整理
• 產業具三大特性：(1)資本密集——12吋線每10萬片/月產能投資約數十億美元；(2)技術密集——12吋拋光片TTV<0.1μm、表
面雜質低於百億分之一；(3)強週期＋寡占——景氣循環約3~4年、擴產週期2年以上，五大廠藉多輪併購形成CR5逾90%格局
，環球晶即為併購成長典範（2012年Covalent、2016年Topsil與SunEdison，市占由第六升至第三）。

### Page 9

9【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
產品以拋光片與磊晶片為主力，SOI單價達4~5倍
資料來源：國信證券、福邦投顧整理
• 矽晶圓依加工深度分為研磨片、拋光片、退火片、磊晶片（外延片）與SOI。拋光片是基準產品，可直接
投產記憶體與功率元件，也是其他高階產品的襯底；磊晶片附加價值高，信越即以磊晶片為主力；SOI具
低功耗、抗輻射優勢，單價為一般矽晶圓的4~5倍，其中RF-SOI（5G射頻前端）占約60%。環球晶為少數
具備全產品線（含SOI、SiC、GaN）的廠商，其美國密蘇里廠即鎖定SOI利基市場。
表2、矽晶圓產品種類比較
種類 製程 用途／特點 價格水準
拋光片 研磨片＋CMP化學機械拋光 記憶體、功率元件；退火／磊晶／SOI之襯底 基準
退火片 拋光片經氫氣／氬氣高溫熱處理 去除表面氧、提高晶體完整性；先進製程用 略高於拋光片1.2倍
磊晶片 拋光片上CVD氣相生長單晶矽層 CPU/GPU、CIS、PMIC、IGBT；缺陷密度更低 高於拋光片1.5~2倍
SOI 氧化埋層＋鍵合／Smart-Cut RF射頻(60%)、功率、車用、星載晶片 約4~5倍

假片／再生片 晶棒次級部位／回收再拋光 設備暖機測試；N5每10萬片正片配約6萬片擋控片 約4~5折以下

• 尺寸大型化是產業長期趨勢：12吋出貨面積占比自2000年
1.7%升至2024年76.4%、2025年約77%，8吋加12吋合計超

• 應用分界在90奈米：90奈米以下（手機SoC、CPU/GPU、
記憶體、AI晶片）用12吋；90奈米以上成熟製程（PMIC
、車用MCU、功率、CIS）以8吋為主；6吋以下則轉向功
率元件與SiC。Siltronic預估矽晶圓長期需求CAGR 5~6%，
成長動能全部來自12吋。

• 五大廠擴產態度分歧：僅環球晶大規模新建，其餘以優化或小幅擴充為主
• 矽晶圓的寡占不是偶然：技術、認證、資金、設備規模四道門檻疊加，讓資金雄厚的新進者也難以速成
。最關鍵的是客戶認證——從送樣到主力供應商一般需5年以上（2年流片評估→1年測試片→1年低量正
片→B/C類→主力），且認證通過後客戶不輕易更換供應商，形成極強黏著度。

### Page 14

14【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
矽晶圓12吋為主流，記憶體為主要成長動能
資料來源：SEMI、材料世界網，福邦投顧整理
• 矽晶圓的終端應用依尺寸分層：12 吋占出貨面積 約75%、
是絕對主流。以晶片類型看，邏輯代工約 55%、DRAM／
HBM /3D NAND 等記憶體合計約 45%。記憶體廠的投片與
庫存決策因此主導矽晶圓的供需與價格，記憶體循環也成為
矽晶圓的領先指標
• 8 吋以 PMIC、車用 MCU 與功率元件為主，隨車用與消費性
回溫在 2H26 復甦；6 吋以下為分離式功率元件，SiC/GaN
亦以 6 吋為主流、逐步轉進 8 吋。AI 需求（HBM、先進邏
輯、CoWoS 矽中介層）全數落在 12 吋，是本輪增量來源。
圖7、12吋矽晶圓消耗結構
55%45% 邏輯記憶體
表6、各尺寸出貨占比、對應製程與終端應用（依出貨面積占比由大至小排列）
尺寸 2025出貨面積占比 對應製程 主要終端應用 2026~28年需求驅動
12吋(300mm) 約75% 90nm以下先進邏輯、DRAM/NAND 手機SoC、CPU/GPU、AI晶片、DRAM/HBM/NAND 記憶體占12吋消耗約45%；AI伺服器（HBM＋先進邏輯＋CoWoS矽中介層）為最大增量

8吋(200mm) 約20% 90nm~0.35μm成熟製程 PMIC、車用MCU、功率元件、CIS、指紋辨識 車用電動化與PMIC回溫，2H26起復甦（信越、Siltronic 7月法說）
6吋以下 約5% 0.35μm以上 二極體、MOSFET、IGBT、MEMS 功率元件；SiC/GaN化合物晶圓以6吋為主流、逐步轉進8吋

• 2026年底缺口約8%：各大晶圓廠去庫存化中，12吋受HBM與先進邏輯製程逐步放量、8吋受PMIC需求回

• 未來產能三大瓶頸：(1)關鍵原物料——中國《兩用物項出口管制清單》限制鎵、鍺、稀土及高純度矽料出
口，為矽料生產、摻雜及外延片製造不可或缺（如信越約15%產能依賴此類上游原料）；(2)製程設備——

高階退火工藝所需氬氣及其製備設備關鍵部件受出口管制，已出現供應短缺；(3)人力吃緊——日韓廠商面
臨高齡化、技術人員短缺，難以調度足夠人力維持與擴大產能。

200+層正片1:0.3）。

25【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
矽晶圓LTA行為是最好的景氣溫度計
資料來源：環球晶法說會、各公司季報，福邦投顧整理
• 矽晶圓無公開報價，主要以3~8年期長約（LTA）搭配現貨交易，客戶常需支付預付款（列合約負債，為訂
單能見度指標）；現貨價與長約價的價差即供需溫度計。指標性事件：2026/7/9美光提供環球晶5億美元策
略資金＋規劃10年期LTA——記憶體廠首度以「策略投資」形式鎖定台廠矽晶圓產能，議價權轉回上游的分
水嶺。

• 各家法說口徑一致轉向：需求端信越指6月300mm出貨創單月新高、各家已滿載並進入產能受限狀態，

Issuer of report: The Hongkong and Shanghai 
Banking Corporation Limited 
View HSBC Global Investment Research at: 
https://www.research.hsbc.com 
The 25th edition of the EM Sentiment Survey
Click to view
Less bull, more bite

◆ Smartphone companies have gone from being demand drivers 
to price takers 
◆ But we think if memory price inflation slows, that could be 
enough for margins to improve before shipments increase 
◆ In a separate report published today, we initiate coverage of 
Xiaomi with a Buy rating and a TP of HKD33.20 
Hope amid hard times. This report looks at what component shortages and higher 
memory prices mean for the economics of the smartphone market. The key message 
is that while average selling prices have jumped as costs surged, handset makers 
don’t need memory prices to fall to recover. Any slowdown in AI-driven memory price 
inflation could be enough for margins to improve before shipments start to increase.  
Stabilisation in 2027, recovery in 2028. IDC forecasts that global smartphone 
shipments will fall 16.7% y-o-y in 2026 – the steepest contraction in industry history – 
decline a further 2.9% in 2027, and rebound 4.5% y-o-y in 2028.  The low base in 2026 
should release some replacement demand but higher handset ASPs and a limited supply 
of low-end memory chips will likely limit the recovery. In China, handset inventories rose 
in 2H25 and 1Q26 before easing in 2Q, suggesting the shock of higher memory costs 
arrived before inventory and demand had normalised. We expect the first green shoots to 
appear in ASPs and gross margin, ahead of shipment numbers. 
Memory inflation hit margins before consumer prices. Spot pricing moves first, 
followed by contract prices, handset makers’ blended procurement costs, component 
inventory consumption, handset bill of materials (BOM) and reported gross margin. 
Lower-cost memory inventory delays the impact on earnings, while finished goods 
and channel inventories delay the ability to reprice. We see 4Q25-1Q26 as the 
margin-absorption phase, with the larger earnings impact likely to run through 2H26.  
Higher costs to be passed on through new products. The main levers for handset 
makers are higher prices for new models, fewer discounts, slower RAM and storage 
upgrades, wider premiums for high-memory models and savings elsewhere in the BOM. 
Times are particularly tough because AI/server demand is tightening memory supply at a 
time when smartphone demand remains weak. In previous cycles, smartphones and PCs 
were major sources of DRAM and NAND demand, so higher memory costs coincided 
with better end demand. This time, handset makers are having to accept supplier-driven 
price increases without the same support from sales volumes.  
MLCC risk. AI servers use more and higher-spec multi-layer ceramic capacitors 
(MLCCs), shifting supply to higher-value products. The effect on handset profits should be 
limited as these key components make up only a small part of the BOM. However, supply 
shortages can still disrupt production – if consumer-grade components are scarce, finding 
replacements takes time. See our Xiaomi initiation report.

Why we think the smartphone market won’t recover in 2027 
The decline in 2026 will not create a strong replacement cycle next year 
According to IDC forecasts made in June 2026, global smartphone shipments will fall 16.7%  
y-o-y in 2026e to just over 1bn units, decline a further 2.9% in 2027e, and then rebound 4.5% in 
2028e when memory supply begins to normalise.  
The low base should support replacement demand, but affordability remains the constraint. Higher 
memory costs are raising the bill of materials (BOM) for handsets and pushing manufacturers to 
either increase prices or reduce entry-level supply. The thinking is that a consumer who defers an 
upgrade in 2026 does not necessarily return in 2027 if the replacement device is materially more 
expensive, or offers less memory at the same price. We think premium markets should be more 
resilient, while entry-level Android demand is more exposed.

Exhibit 2. Global smartphone shipments: 
recovery pushed out to 2028e 
 Exhibit 3. China smartphone inventories* 
were still elevated when memory costs 
began to rise

Source: IDC estimates, HSBC  *Inventory proxy: China’s smartphone production + imports – China’s smartphone 
shipment – exports. 
Source: NBS, GAC, CAICT, HSBC

The first-half vendor data already show this split. Global shipments declined 5.5% y-o-y in 1H26. 
Apple and Samsung shipments rose 5.8% and 8.7%, respectively, while those for Xiaomi, 
OPPO and Vivo fell 22.8%, 13.8% and 13.5%, respectively (source: IDC). Memory is not the 
only explanation: product timing, geography, channel execution and inventory also matter. 
However, the divergence is consistent with stronger resilience at the premium end and greater 
volume pressure in price-sensitive Android segments.  
China’s implied handset-inventory series point in the same direction. Inventory rose through 
2H25 and reached another high in 1Q26 before easing in 2Q26. The series is calculated as 
production plus imports less domestic shipments and exports. It should be treated as a 
directional supply-chain proxy rather than actual channel inventory because the underlying 
datasets use different definitions and timing. The turning point is nevertheless useful: 
manufacturers entered the memory upcycle before the finished-goods cycle had fully normalised. 
Memory pressure extends beyond smartphones, but the earnings sensitivity varies 
materially by product 
TVs have seen a meaningful increase in memory’s share of BOM as prices rose, while 
wearables, audio devices and other IoT products generally carry lower absolute memory 
content and therefore less direct P&L sensitivity. In these categories, selected niche-memory 
availability and channel inventory & pricing can matter more than headline DRAM or NAND 
moves. We are beginning to see signs that pricing momentum is moderating in some consumer 
and niche categories, although conditions remain tight in higher-capacity products. Our sector

Higher memory prices do not directly determine smartphone 
shipments 
Why memory inflation is not the sole cause of the fall in smartphone demand 
Xiaomi’s historical shipment data suggests that the relationship is weak. Mobile DRAM prices 
have moved through several cycles since 2018, while Xiaomi’s quarterly shipments have been 
driven more visibly by product launches and seasonality. Falling memory prices have not 
consistently triggered stronger shipments, while rising prices have not resulted in an immediate 
or proportionate decline in units.
